# Competitor backlog implementation tracker

Started 2026-10-28. User authorized implementation of the full 57-item research backlog and the sol-loop skill; current desktop completion scope is macOS only. Latest model instruction: GPT only; no Anthropic models. Every model uses Standard service, never Fast/Priority/ultrafast. Historical model instructions and reviews below describe earlier work. This is a staged implementation effort; source presence, completed code, passing checks and physical/human qualification are tracked separately.

Branch: `feat/competitor-backlog-2026-10-28`. Task baseline: `6a8d771c72a335fe8fdecee2889906785eb6d050`. Fetched origin; remote merge-base: `3670716607d9d2e634f35ed6c0dca357f1faa3a9`. Earlier local commits are outside this task. Task/review diff uses current task baseline `6a8d771c72a335fe8fdecee2889906785eb6d050` plus explicitly scoped new files, preserving those commits and pre-existing working-tree work.

Preserved pre-existing work: README.md modifications; untracked android/ implementation; docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-mobile-cross-app-dictation.md; research artifacts in docs/research/2026-10-28/. Do not stash/discard these or include them in a task-only commit/review. Android will be changed deliberately only in its implementation wave, with its baseline captured before edits. No commit/push/PR requested.

## Execution rules

- One code-writing agent at a time in the shared checkout. No application edits while the relevant investigation/review is running. Existing B49 blind/refutation audits and root symbol inspection confirmed the pre-fix paths; source is unchanged since that evidence.
- Routine coding/research uses Codex · pritam (`codex`, `gpt-6.1-sol`, reasoning high, explicit `serviceTier: default`). GPT models only under the latest instruction. Every model uses Standard only; never inherit the GPT catalog Priority default and never enable Fast/Priority/ultrafast.
- Isolated macOS builds are explicitly authorized by the user. Prefer focused test commands and suitable type/lint checks. No browser/device flood, dependency installs or model downloads just for read-only planning. Install pinned dependencies only when implementation requires them. Parakeet signing remains deferred at the user's request.
- sol-loop: one independent GPT Standard reviewer per round; no reviewer subagents; default three rounds, up to five only for evidenced state/concurrency/security/data-loss findings. Each round is a fresh async delegate. Freeze scoped files until notice, keep a finding ledger, and do not treat timeout as approval.
- Experimental context/audio/provider features remain explicit opt-ins. No silent cloud ASR, default model migrations, screen capture or retained canceled audio. The user's broad feature authorization does not fabricate consented human data or physical-device test results.

### Wave 2A implementation contract

Blind investigation complete: `/tmp/parla-implementation-2026-10-28/wave2-investigation.md`; all 74 snapshotted files unchanged during the audit; no hard blocker. Root independently inspected main key-down/settings/model path, Dictation stream/final/command/landing paths, session settings, transcriber language state, category routing and Hub persistence/navigation.

Adopted defaults: no profiles on existing installations; exact host > exact bundle ID > globals; one winning rule with unset fields inherited; first saved enabled duplicate wins deterministically while editor rejects duplicate targets; URL matching local and host-only. Recognition winner frozen at capture start for language/model/vocabulary across every ASR pass; output winner frozen at destination resolution before cleanup for mode/style/model/vocabulary. This preserves the deliberate destination-changing workflow. Terminal/chat/single-line and secure/destination identity constraints still win. Cleanup-off retains existing locked snippets/literal replacement behavior. Provider credentials remain global. No implicit downloads or model fallback that misreports which model ran.

Implement only B03 and remaining B04 app priority in this slice. Saved transforms, history/recovery/export, layout display, engine expansion, cancellation and other waves remain staged. One Pritam writer; root does not edit application files during the worker. Focused fake-client/model-free tests must cover production seams as well as helpers. Native app/site/model switching and live accuracy remain explicit later qualification.

Retry is explicit request → result preview → conditional save. Keep raw immutable. Show source/previous/proposed text and current selected request/provider/model/style/language/mode/vocabulary summary before calling provider; safe endpoint origin only, no credentials or full URL secrets. Legacy provenance unknown, never inferred from current settings. Use explicit current global retry configuration and disclose absent historical app/site context rather than sampling Hub focus. Ordinary cleanup versus intentional current-preset transformation must be separately named/intentional; never silently replay a spoken transform whose instruction was not stored.

Freeze request/settings/token per row; new request invalidates older completion for that row. Deletion, clear, eviction or changed row cannot be resurrected/overwritten by completion or Retry Save. Retain a successful result before saving; Apply/Retry Save only persists the same result, zero new provider calls. No insertion/selection/capture/ASR capability in the retry service. Provider failures require explicit new request, not storage retry. Reuse shared cleanup/local-rule/intentional-transform semantics and operation-owned usage sink. Safe provenance and actual resolved model may be stored for new retry results; no credential-bearing settings snapshots.

History-off supports temporary preview/Copy. Explicit Save is disabled while off; enabling history lets the retained result be explicitly saved without a new request. Existing original remains readable. Native dialogs/layout/model fidelity remain separately unqualified. No export/audio/recovery/layout/later-wave work in this slice.
